spec_law_mult = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { is_country_type = decentralized }
		value = 0
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_laissez_faire }
		value = 1.5
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_interventionism }
		value = 0.7
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_extraction_economy }
		value = 1
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_agrarianism }
		value = 0.5
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_traditionalism }
		value = 0.5
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_industry_banned }
		value = 0.3
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_command_economy }
		value = 0
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_cooperative_ownership }
		value = 0
	}
}

spec_band_gain = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_conf }
		if = {
			limit = { var:spec_conf < 40 }
			value = 0.8
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { var:spec_conf >= 80 }
			value = 1.3
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { var:spec_conf >= 60 }
			value = 1.15
		}
	}
}

spec_credit = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_conf }
		add = var:spec_conf
	}
	subtract = 55
	divide = 45
	multiply = 1.5
	max = 0.4
	multiply = spec_pool_month
	multiply = spec_credit_rate
	max = spec_credit_ceiling
	max = spec_credit_room
	min = 0
}

spec_credit_preview = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_conf }
		add = var:spec_conf
	}
	subtract = 55
	divide = 45
	multiply = 1.5
	max = 0.4
	multiply = spec_pool_month
	multiply = spec_law_mult
	multiply = spec_tech_credit
	multiply = spec_reform_credit
	multiply = spec_lax_credit
	multiply = spec_exch_credit
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { has_variable = spec_panic has_variable = spec_crunch has_modifier = spec_ld_crash AND = { has_variable = spec_conf var:spec_conf < spec_panic_at } } }
		value = 0
	}
	max = spec_credit_ceiling
	max = spec_credit_room
	min = 0
}

spec_lev = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_debt
			spec_lev_income > 1
		}
		value = var:spec_debt
		divide = spec_lev_income
		min = 0
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_debt
			var:spec_debt > 0
			has_variable = spec_lev_last
		}
		value = var:spec_lev_last
	}
}

	value = spec_lev

		add = {
			value = var:spec_lev_old
			divide = var:spec_lev_ref
		}
		max = spec_lev
		min = 0
	}
}

		value = 2
	}
}

			has_variable = spec_restrain_electricity
			has_variable = spec_hype_electricity
		}
		max = { value = var:spec_hype_electricity subtract = 1 multiply = 0.65 add = 1 }
		min = 1
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_encourage_electricity
			has_variable = spec_hype_electricity
		}
		min = { value = 2.2 subtract = var:spec_hype_electricity multiply = 0.35 add = var:spec_hype_electricity max = 2 }
	}
}

spec_peak_next_electricity = {
	value = var:spec_peak_electricity
	multiply = 0.97
	add = 0.03
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_electricity }
		value = var:spec_peak_electricity
		multiply = 0.9
		add = 0.1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_hype_electricity > var:spec_peak_electricity }
		value = var:spec_hype_electricity
	}
}

	value = var:spec_peak_electricity
	subtract = spec_peak_next_electricity

	multiply = 1.25
	multiply = spec_weight_electricity
	multiply = spec_exp_mult_electricity

		multiply = spec_exp_mult_manzoni_prints
		multiply = spec_over_mult_manzoni_prints

		multiply = 2.5
		multiply = spec_weight_manzoni_prints

	multiply = spec_crash_scale
	max = 40
}

spec_dev_porcelain = {
	value = 0
	market ?= {
		mg:porcelain ?= {
			add = market_goods_pricier
		}
	}
}

spec_mv_porcelain = {
	value = 0
	market ?= {
		mg:porcelain ?= {
			add = market_goods_sell_orders
		}
	}
	multiply = 70
}

spec_weight_porcelain = {
	value = spec_mv_porcelain
	divide = var:spec_mv_total
	divide = 0.03
	max = 1
}

spec_fast_next_porcelain = {
	value = var:spec_fast_porcelain
	multiply = 0.85
	add = {
		value = spec_dev_porcelain
		multiply = 0.15
	}
}

spec_slow_next_porcelain = {
	value = var:spec_slow_porcelain
	multiply = 0.95
	add = {
		value = spec_dev_porcelain
		multiply = 0.05
	}
}

spec_trend_porcelain = {
	value = var:spec_fast_porcelain
	subtract = var:spec_slow_porcelain
	multiply = 60
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_fast_porcelain >= var:spec_slow_porcelain }
		multiply = var:spec_hm
	}
	else = {
		multiply = var:spec_hmd
	}
}

spec_hype_raw_porcelain = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { spec_trend_porcelain >= 0 }
		add = spec_trend_porcelain
	}
	else = {
		divide = {
			value = 1
			subtract = spec_trend_porcelain
		}
	}
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
}

spec_hype_next_porcelain = {
	value = spec_hype_raw_porcelain
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_glut_porcelain }
		value = 0.4
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_restrain_porcelain
			has_variable = spec_hype_porcelain
		}
		max = { value = var:spec_hype_porcelain subtract = 1 multiply = 0.65 add = 1 }
		min = 1
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_encourage_porcelain
			has_variable = spec_hype_porcelain
		}
		min = { value = 2.2 subtract = var:spec_hype_porcelain multiply = 0.35 add = var:spec_hype_porcelain max = 2 }
	}
}

spec_peak_next_porcelain = {
	value = var:spec_peak_porcelain
	multiply = 0.97
	add = 0.03
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_porcelain }
		value = var:spec_peak_porcelain
		multiply = 0.9
		add = 0.1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_hype_porcelain > var:spec_peak_porcelain }
		value = var:spec_hype_porcelain
	}
}

spec_restrain_cost_porcelain = {
	value = var:spec_peak_porcelain
	subtract = spec_peak_next_porcelain
	min = 0
	multiply = 1.25
	multiply = spec_weight_porcelain
	multiply = spec_exp_mult_porcelain
}

spec_sev_next_porcelain = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_porcelain >= 6 }
		value = 3
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_porcelain >= 3 }
		value = 2
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_porcelain >= 2 }
		value = 1
	}
}

spec_risk_raw_porcelain = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_porcelain >= 6 }
		value = var:spec_peak_porcelain
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 6
		multiply = spec_weight_porcelain
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_porcelain
		multiply = spec_over_mult_porcelain
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_porcelain >= 3 }
		value = var:spec_peak_porcelain
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 2.5
		multiply = spec_weight_porcelain
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_porcelain
		multiply = spec_over_mult_porcelain
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_porcelain >= 2 }
		value = var:spec_peak_porcelain
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 1.25
		multiply = spec_weight_porcelain
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_porcelain
		multiply = spec_over_mult_porcelain
	}
}

spec_risk_next_porcelain = {
	value = spec_risk_raw_porcelain
	multiply = spec_crash_scale
	max = 40
}

	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_wood >= 3 }
		value = 2
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_wood >= 2 }
		value = 1
	}
}

spec_risk_raw_wood = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_wood >= 6 }
		value = var:spec_peak_wood
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 6
		multiply = spec_weight_wood
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_wood
		multiply = spec_over_mult_wood
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_wood >= 3 }
		value = var:spec_peak_wood
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 2.5
		multiply = spec_weight_wood
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_wood
		multiply = spec_over_mult_wood
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_wood >= 2 }
		value = var:spec_peak_wood
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 1.25
		multiply = spec_weight_wood
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_wood
		multiply = spec_over_mult_wood
	}
}

spec_risk_next_wood = {
	value = spec_risk_raw_wood
	multiply = spec_crash_scale
	max = 40
}
